<SCRIPT language=VBScript event=onclick for=shape009_group001 defer>	dim sShow,sErr,oShape
	on error resume next
	sShow=window.event.srcelement.value
	sErr=window.external.dictionary.item("NameUnitsHidden").value
	if err.number=0 then
 		window.external.dictionary.item("NameUnitsHidden").value=sShow
	else
 		window.external.dictionary.add "NameUnitsHidden",sShow
	end if

	for each oShape in Page.children.tags("DIV")
  		if oShape.classname="hsc.shape.1" then
 			if typename(oShape.objects("TxtName"))<>"Nothing" then
    			call Butt_Page_OnShapeLoad(oShape,sShow)
 			end if
  		end if
	next

	bFP=false
	set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("1oo1")
	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true
	if bFP=false then set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("1oo2")
	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true
	if bFP=false then set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("2oo2")
	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true
	if bFP=false then set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("2oo2")
	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true
	if bFP=false then set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("2oo3")
	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true

	if bFP=true then
    	for each oShape in oPop.document.all.page.children.tags("DIV")
  			if oShape.classname="hsc.shape.1" then
 				if typename(oShape.objects("TxtName"))<>"Nothing" then
    				call Butt_Page_OnShapeLoad(oShape,sShow)
 				end if
  			end if
		next
	end if</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=723F5936-2443-4ADE-A742-C4D4D1685491 defer
shaperefcount="1">sub Butt_Page_OnShapeLoad(oSource, sShow)
on error resume next
set txtName = oSource.objects("TxtName")
set txtEUDesc = oSource.objects("TxtEUDesc")

if sShow="Имя" then
  txtName.style.visibility="inherit"
  txtEUDesc.style.visibility="hidden"
elseif sShow="ИзмЕ" then
  txtName.style.visibility="hidden"
  txtEUDesc.style.visibility="inherit"
else
  txtName.style.visibility="hidden"
  txtEUDesc.style.visibility="hidden"
end if

sTagEU = oSource.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")
if sTagEU <> "-" then
	if sTagEU<> "" then
		if instr(sTagEU,",")<>0 then
			sTag = left(sTagEU,instr(sTagEU,",")-1)
			sEU	= ltrim(mid(sTagEU,instr(sTagEU,",")+1))
		elseif instr(sTagEU,",")=1 then
			sTag = ""
			sEU	= ltrim(mid(sTagEU,instr(sTagEU,",")+1))
		else
			sTag = sTagEU
			sEU	= ""
		end if
		txtEUDesc.innertext = sEU
	else
		if instr(oSource.src,"PU_")>0 then
			sTag=oSource.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
        else
		    sTag = oSource.getcustomproperty("Point","Tagname")
        end if

		if oSource.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_eudesc")<>"" then sEU = oSource.objects("ScConAlpPv").datavalue("Tagname.cp_eudesc")
	end if
	txtName.innertext = sTag
	txtEUDesc.innertext = sEU
else
	txtName.style.visibility="hidden"
  	txtEUDesc.style.visibility="hidden"
end if


end sub</SCRIPT>

  		end if
	next
	bFP=false

	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true
	if bFP=false then set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("1oo2")
	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true
	if bFP=false then set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("2oo2")
	if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then bFP=true

	if bFP=true then

 				if typename(oShape.objects("TxtName"))<>"Nothing" then

	end if</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=303B75FD-599B-4323-B9BD-DE498428A678 defer
shaperefcount="1">dim tid_timeout,sAlarmGroupName, bCancel, bTrdResp, Xorig, Yorig
dim oPrntSrc

const  iQualityGood = 192
const  iQualityOffScan = 8
const  iQualityBad = 4
const  iQualityBadComm = 20
const  iQualityAccessNotPermitted = 1024


tid_timeout = 0 	'Used by Com_GraphTitle.sha. Counts the time before resetting to originally configured alarm group.
sAlarmGroupName="" 	'Used by Com_GraphTitle.sha. Stores the alarm group name as configured in the shape.

'Allow only tags of type INPUT and TEXTAREA to be selected.
sub Page_OnSelectStart
	dim InpName
	on error resume next
	InpName=window.event.SrcElement.TagName
	if InpName <> "INPUT" and InpName<>"TEXTAREA" then window.event.returnvalue = false
end sub


'Show the appropriate tooltip
sub Page_OnMouseOver
	dim oObj, iNrPoints,sPointNames(20),sCPNames(20)
	on error resume next
	set oobj=window.event.srcelement
	if isobject (oObj.onmouseover) then exit sub
	if len(oObj.id)<14 then exit sub
	if ucase(right(oObj.id,14))="SCTXTSELECTBOX" then
		set oShape = oObj.parentnode.parentnode
 		oObj.style.cursor = "hand"
 		if oShape.getcustomproperty("point","tagname")<>"" then
 			for n=0 to oShape.customProperties.Count -1
 			   if lcase(oShape.customProperties.Item(n).type) = "point" and instr(oShape.customProperties.Item(n).name,"Analog")=0 then
				  iNrPoints = iNrPoints + 1
 				  sPointNames(iNrPoints) =	oshape.customProperties.Item(n).value
 				  sCPNames(iNrPoints) =	oshape.customProperties.Item(n).name
    		   end if
 			next
			if instr(oShape.src,"MultBPIL")>0 then iNrPoints=1
  			If iNrPoints = 1 Then
  	 			sPoints = sPointNames(1)
				if inStr(sPoints,"{%")>0 then sPoints=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("Tagname.name")
	  			status = "Analog"
				if instr(oShape.src,"MultBPIL")>0 or (oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc") is nothing) or (not oShape.objects("TxtPV") is nothing) or (not oShape.objects("RecPV") is nothing) then status = "Status"
      			if (not oShape.objects("IndVal") is nothing or not oShape.objects("TxtOP")is nothing) then status = "RegValve"
                if (not oShape.objects("LinScale") is nothing) then status = "Analog"
				if status = "Analog" then
					PvTxt = ToolTip_PV(oShape,status)
					SpTxt = ToolTip_SP(oShape,status)
					OpTxt = ToolTip_OP(oShape)
					ModeTxt = ToolTip_Mode(oShape)
					AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape,"")
					sToolTip= sPoints & vbCR & SPTxt  & PVTxt & OpTxt &  ModeTxt & AlarmTxt
					if AlarmTxt <> "" then
						oObj.title = sToolTip
					else
						oObj.title = mid(sToolTip,1,len(sToolTip)-1)
					end if
	  			elseif status = "RegValve" then
	  				PVTxt = ToolTip_PV(oShape,status)
					OPTxt = ToolTip_OP(oShape)
					AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape,"")
					sToolTip= sPoints & vbCR & PVTxt  & OPTxt & AlarmTxt
					if AlarmTxt <> "" then
						oObj.title = sToolTip
					else
						oObj.title = mid(sToolTip,1,len(sToolTip)-1)
					end if
	  			else
					AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape,"")
					sToolTip = sPoints & vbcr &  PVTxt &  AlarmTxt
					if AlarmTxt <> "" then
						oObj.title = sToolTip
					else
						oObj.title = mid(sToolTip,1,len(sToolTip)-1)
					end if
	  			end if
  			Elseif iNrPoints > 1 Then
				if instr(sPointNames(1),"{%")>0 then
					sPoints=ucase(oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue(sCPNames(1)&".name")) & vbcr
				else
					sPoints = ucase(sPointNames(1)) & vbcr
				end if
				For i=2 to iNrPoints
				    if instr(sPointNames(i),"{%")>0 then
						sPoints=sPoints & vbcr & ucase(oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue(sCPNames(i)&".name"))
					else
						sPoints = sPoints & vbcr & ucase(sPointNames(i))
					end if
					AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape, i-1)
					if AlarmTxt <> "" and typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & i-1))<>"Nothing" then
						if oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & i-1).style.visibility = "inherit" then sAlm = AlarmTxt
					end if
     			Next
				if sAlm<>"" then sPoints = sPoints & vbcr & vbcr & sAlm
				oObj.title= sPoints
  			End If
			if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","FaceplateFile")="-" then
				oObj.style.cursor="default"
			else
				oObj.style.cursor="hand"
			end if
			if oShape.getcustomproperty("text","faceplatefile")="-" then oShape.objects("sctxtselectbox").style.cursor="default"
   		end if
 	elseif ucase(right(oObj.id,14))="SCTXTSELECTION" then
    	set oShape=oObj.parentnode.parentnode
		oShape.objects("ScTxtSelection").style.cursor="hand"
 	end if
end sub

'Get the PV Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_PV (oShape,PointType)
	on error resume next
	select case PointType
		case "Analog"
			sPV = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_pv")
			sPos = instr(sPV,".")
			if spos>0 then sPV = mid(sPV,sPos+1,len(sPv)-sPos)
    		EUDesc = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
			PVFormat = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat")
			if PVFormat="" then PVFormat = 2
			PVFormat = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_pvformat")
			PV = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value
			If isnumeric(PV) and sPV<>"" Then
				PVTxt = ucase(sPV) & "="  & FormatNumber (PV, PVFormat) & " " & EuDesc & vbcr
			elseif sPV<>"" then
				PVTxt =ucase(sPV) & "="  & PV & vbcr
			end if
		case "RegValve"
			sPV = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_pv")
			sPos = instr(sPV,".")
			if spos>0 then sPV = mid(sPV,sPos+1,len(sPv)-sPos)
	    	PV = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value
	    	If sPV<>"" Then PVTxt = ucase(sPV) & "="  & FormatNumber (PV, 0) & " " & "%" & vbcr
	end select
	ToolTip_PV= PVTxt
end function

'Get the SP Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_SP (oShape,PointType)
	on error resume next
	select case PointType
		case "Analog"
			sSP = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_sp")
			sPos = instr(sSP,".")
			if spos>0 then sSP = mid(sSP,sPos+1,len(sSP)-sPos)
			if sSP = "" and oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_dev") <>"" then sSP = "SP"
			SP=""
			SP = oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").value
			SP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("Tagname.cp_sp")
			SP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value - oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_dev")
			PVFormat = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat")
			if PVFormat="" then PVFormat = 2
			PVFormat = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_pvformat")
			EUDesc = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
			If isnumeric(SP) Then SPTxt = vblf & ucase(sSP) & "="  & FormatNumber (SP, PVFormat) & " " & EuDesc & vbcr
	end select
	ToolTip_SP= SPTxt
end function

'Get the OP Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_OP(oShape)',PointType)
	on error resume next
			sOP = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_op")
			sPos = instr(sOP,".")
			if spos>0 then sOP = mid(sOP,sPos+1,len(sOP)-sPos)
        	PVFormat=1
			OP=""
			OP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value
			OP = oShape.objects("ConAlpOP").value
			OP = oShape.objects("ConIndOP").value
			OP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpOP").datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
	    	If sOP<>"" Then OPTxt = ucase(sOP) & "="  & FormatNumber (OP, PVFormat) & " " & "%" & vbcr
	ToolTip_OP = OPTxt
end function

'Get the Mode Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_Mode(oShape)
	on error resume next
	ModeTxt = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
	if len(ModeTxt)>0 Then ToolTip_Mode = "Mode= " & ModeTxt &vbcr
end function

'Get the Alarm Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_Alm (oShape,index)
	on error resume next
	if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index))<> "Nothing" then
		AlarmTxt = oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index).title
		IF len(AlarmTxt)<>0 and instr(AlarmTxt,"Quality")=0 Then
			ToolTip_Alm = "Alarm= " & AlarmTxt
		elseif index="" then
			ToolTip_Alm = "No Alarm "
		else
			 ToolTip_Alm=""
		end if
	end if
end function

'Focus ConAlarmState if an alarm is present or ScConAlpPV when there is no alarm.
sub ObjSel
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=document.getelementbyID(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	set oAlarmIcon = nothing
  	for each oAlarmState in oShape.children(0).all
    	if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 and (oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit" or oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "")  then
			if instr(oAlarmState.title,"Unack") or instr(ucase(page.getattribute("lastselection")),"ALM_SCTXTSELECTBOX")>0 and oAlarmState.title<>"" then
				set oAlarmIcon = oAlarmState
			end if
    	end if
  	next
  	if oAlarmIcon is nothing Then
		for each oAlphaNum in oShape.all.tags("TEXTAREA")
			if (oAlphaNum.alarm and &H400)=&H400 then
				set oAlarmIcon=oAlphaNum
				exit for
			end if
  		next
    end if
  	if oAlarmIcon is nothing Then
    	oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV").focus
  	else
    	oAlarmIcon.focus
  	end if
end sub

'Provide Left Click behavior. Invoke Faceplate
sub All_OnClick(oSource)
	dim oShape,soldsel
  	on error resume next
	set oPrntSrc=window.external
	if bTrdResp=true then bCancel=true	' Check to prevent message zone flickering, when All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is not present in the current display.
										' See Trend_Sup_GSH.sha trdresp subroutine
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
    set otarget=oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV")
    sfaceplatefile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","faceplatefile")
    if sfaceplatefile="" then
   	   set oSource.onfocus = nothing
       otarget.click
	   oTarget.style.color = oTarget.textcolor
    elseif sfaceplatefile="-" then
       if not isobject(oSource.onfocus) then set oSource.onfocus=getref("ObjSel")
       oSource.focus
    else
       scurrentpoint=oshape.getcustomproperty("point","tagname")
       sPoints= "?CurrentPoint=" & sCurrentPoint
       for n=1 to oshape.customProperties.Count -1
  	      if oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Point" or oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Parameter" then
    	    sPoints = sPoints & "&" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).name & "=" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).value
	      end if
		  if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat")<>"" then sPoints=sPoints & "&cp_pvformat=" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat")
       next
       sfaceplatefile = mid(sfaceplatefile,1,instr(1,sfaceplatefile,".htm")+3)
	   if oTarget.quality = 0  then
	   		if ucase(Left(oShape.customproperties.Item(1).Name,3))="TAG" then
				Quality = oTarget.DataQuality(oShape.customproperties.Item(1).Name & ".cp_Tag1_pv" ) ' Used for F&G Shapes
			else
				Quality = oTarget.DataQuality(oShape.customproperties.Item(1).Name & ".cp_AlmPar1" ) ' Used for Multialarm shapes
			end if
	   else
	   		Quality = oTarget.quality
	   end if
       if instr(sfaceplatefile,"sysPUP")>0 then
	   		ifpmode=0
			set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("1oo1")
			if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then oPop.close
			set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("1oo2")
			if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then oPop.close
			set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("2oo2")
			if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then oPop.close
			set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("2oo2")
			if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then oPop.close
			set oPop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("2oo3")
			if typename(oPop)<>"Nothing" then oPop.close
	   else
	   		ifpmode=1
	   end if
       if (Quality and iQualityAccessNotPermitted) <> iQualityAccessNotPermitted then call hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow(sfaceplatefile & spoints,0,0,ifpmode)
    end if
end sub

sub All_MultInt_OnClick(oSource)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	window.external.currentpage=oshape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_CEDisplay")
end sub

sub All_MultAlm_OnClick(oSource)
	dim oShape,soldsel
  	on error resume next
 	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
  	for each oAlarmState in oShape.children(0).all
    	if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 and oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit" then
			set oTarget = oAlarmState
    	end if
  	next
    sfaceplatefile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","faceplatefile" & right(otarget.id,1))
    if sfaceplatefile="" then
   	   set oSource.onfocus = nothing
       otarget.click
    elseif sfaceplatefile="-" then
       if not isobject(oSource.onfocus) then set oSource.onfocus=getref("ObjSel")
       oSource.focus
    else
       scurrentpoint=oshape.getcustomproperty("point","tagname")
       sPoints= "?CurrentPoint=" & sCurrentPoint
       for n=1 to oshape.customProperties.Count -1
  	      if oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Point" or oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Parameter" then
    	    sPoints = sPoints & "&" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).name & "=" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).value
	      end if
       next
       sfaceplatefile = mid(sfaceplatefile,1,instr(1,sfaceplatefile,".htm")+3)
       if (Quality and iQualityAccessNotPermitted) <> iQualityAccessNotPermitted then call hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow(sfaceplatefile & spoints,0,0,1)
    end if
end sub

sub All_MultAlm_OnCMenu(oSource)
	dim oShape,soldsel
  	on error resume next
 	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
  	for each oAlarmState in oShape.children(0).all
    	if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 and oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit" then
			set oTarget = oAlarmState
    	end if
  	next
   	set oSource.onfocus = nothing
    sCMfile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","ContextMenuFile" & right(otarget.id,1))
    if isnull(page.getattribute("CMenu_GSH")) then
		use_cm_shape=false
	else
		use_cm_shape = true
	end if
	if not isobject(oSource.onfocus) then set oSource.onfocus=getref("ObjSel")
    if sCMfile="-" then
      oSource.focus
    elseif sCMfile="" or use_cm_shape = false then
      oTarget.fireevent("oncontextmenu")
      oSource.focus
    else
      hdxpopupbehavior.invokeshortcutmenu sCMfile,0,0
      oSource.focus
    end if
end sub

'Provide Right Click behavior. Invoke Context Menu.
sub All_OnCMenu(oSource)
    dim oShape,soldsel
    on error resume next
    if bTrdResp=true then bCancel=true	' Check to prevent message zone flickering, when All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is not present in the current display.
										' See Trend_Sup_GSH.sha trdresp subroutine
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
    if not isobject(oSource.onactivate) then set oSource.onactivate=getref("ObjSel")
    sCMfile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","ContextMenuFile")
    if isnull(page.getattribute("CMenu_GSH")) then
		use_cm_shape=false
	else
		use_cm_shape = true
	end if
    if sCMfile="-" then
      oSource.focus
    elseif sCMfile="" or use_cm_shape = false then
      oSource.focus
	  oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").fireevent("oncontextmenu")
    else
	  oSource.focus
      hdxpopupbehavior.invokeshortcutmenu sCMfile,0,0
    end if
	if oShape.object("ScConAlpPV").style.pixelwidth>0 then
		if not isobject(oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").onblur) then set oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").onblur=getref("CM_focus")
    end if
end sub

sub CM_Focus
    on error resume next
    soldsel=page.getattribute("lastselection")
	set oShape=document.getelementbyid(soldsel).parentnode.parentnode
	oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass
	oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.color=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").textcolor
	oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.backgroundcolor=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").fillcolor
end sub

'Change the ScTxtSelectBox color when selected
sub SelectBox_Color(oSource)
	dim soldsel
	on error resume next
	soldsel=page.getattribute("lastselection")
	if not isnull(soldsel) then page.all.item(soldsel).styleclass = "ScTxtSelectBox_NotSelected"
    page.setattribute "lastselection",osource.id
    if ucase(right(oSource.id,18)) <> "ALM_SCTXTSELECTBOX" then osource.styleclass = "ScTxtSelectBox_Selected"
end sub

'Determine the most importatnt alarm. Used in shapes with multiple tagnames
Sub Multiple_Alarm_Icon(oSource)
	Dim sMostImportantAlarm, sAlarmState, iThisImportancy, iMostImportant, oAlarmState, iAlarmCount, iInActiveAlarmCount
	On error resume next
	iMostImportant=0
	for each oAlarmState in oSource.parentnode.children
		if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 then
 			iAlarmCount = iAlarmCount + 1
    		iThisImportancy = 0
    		iThisImportancy = 24+oAlarmState.Acknowledged*16 + oAlarmState.Priority*2 + oAlarmState.RTN*8
    		if iThisImportancy  = 16 then iThisImportancy = 8
    		if iThisImportancy >iMostImportant then
       			sMostImportantAlarm = oAlarmState.id
       			iMostImportant = iThisImportancy
    		end if
			if iThisImportancy = 0 then iInActiveAlarmCount = iInActiveAlarmCount + 1
 		end if
	next
	for each oAlarmState in oSource.parentnode.children
   		if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 then
      		if oAlarmState.id = sMostImportantAlarm or iAlarmCount = iInActiveAlarmCount then
 	  	 		oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit"
      		else
         		oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "hidden"
      		end if
    	end if
	next
End Sub

sub Nav_SelectBox_OnClick(oSource)
	on error resume next
	window.external.currentpage = oSource.parentnode.parentnode.getcustomproperty("Text","DspName")
end sub

Sub NavsArrow_HP_AlpAlarm_OnDataChange(oSource)
	dim oShape,iUnack,iAck
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	oShape.Objects("HprLnkDsp").style.color=page.GetStyleClassProperty("TagNameEUDesc", "hw-text-color")
	iPr=(oSource.dataalarm("tagname.name") and 48)/16
	iUnack=(oSource.dataalarm("tagname.name") and 1024)=1024
	if iUnack = True then
	  	oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=true
	else
   		oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=false
	end if
	select case iPr
    case 0
		if oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=true then
			oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").styleclass = "NavFrame_Unack"
		else
		 	oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").styleclass = "NavFrame"
		end if
	case 1
		oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconfactory.lowColor
	case 2
		oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconfactory.highColor
	case 3
		oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconfactory.urgentColor
	end select
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " NavsArrow_HP_AlpAlarm_OnDataChange ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub All_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oPV=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV")
  	iPV=oSource.breakpointvalueenum
   	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   	oPV.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " All_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub All_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oPVObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oPVObj=oShape.objects("TxtPV")
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad and  (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)<>iQualityOffScan then
      oPVObj.innertext="--------"
      oPVObj.textcolor=vbRed
   else
	  oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
      oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" & cstr(oSource.value))
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " All_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'Interlock and Bypass
'-------------------------------------
sub All_ILBP_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape, iPV
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iValueONInt = cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_PlgInterlock_ONValue"))
	if instr(oSource.value,"#")=0 then if iValueONInt=cint(oSource.value) then : iPV=1 : else : iPV=0 : end if
	if (oSource.quality and 192)<>192 then iPV=2
	index = oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_Style" & iPV)
    oShape.objects("PlgInterLock").styleclass = "PlgInterLock_" & index
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_ILBP_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub</SCRIPT>

	if bTrendset = false then

			window.external.Dictionary.Add "TrendNo", 1

		else
			me.value = TrendNo
		end if

	end if
	window.external.Dictionary.Item("TrendNo").value=me.value
	window.settimeout "is_empty", 200

	end if

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=2323F590-AA88-4454-AE52-8E4F915B2FB7 defer
shaperefcount="1">'This shape provides the ability to view, edit and invoke trend groups.
dim tid				'Timer ID
dim bGroupSelected	'Used if All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is missing to ensure the operator responce
dim sParameter		'Used to store the parameter to be added in the selected trend group
dim bLoaded			'Used to to ensure once off update of the chart shape if present
dim oWindow_Tnd			'Used to reload trend in SafeView Environment when trace added
dim selrecoffset	'Used to reload trend in SafeView Environment when trace added
dim bTrendSet		'Used to set the trend number to the last selected trend

'Check the number of empty pens left in the currently selected trend group. Called within AlpTrendGrp.onupdate
sub is_empty
	dim tagn, e_pl, pointpar, oShape, oShape_sel
	on error resume next
	if isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel")) then exit sub
	set oshape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	set oshape_sel=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("trdsel"))
	if oShape.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and window.external.securitylevel<4 then
   		oShape_sel.title = "Protected"
		if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Protected"
		if oshape.objects("TrdTbl").style.visibility = "visible" then oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=400
		exit sub
	else
		oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=0
	end if
	e_pl =0
	for i= 0 to 31
		pointpar = oShape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		if pointpar=" " then e_pl=e_pl+1
	next
	oShape_sel.title = "Free Pens " & e_pl
	if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Free Pens " & e_pl
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "is_empty ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'Update the contents of the chart shape object with the pens in the currently selected trend group. Called within AlpTrendGrp.onupdate
sub upd_chart
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	if isnull(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH")) then exit sub
	if isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel")) then exit sub
	set oshape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	for each oObj in page.all.tags("DIV")
  		if oObj.classname="hsc.shape.1" then
     		if not (oObj.objects("Custtrd") is nothing) then
				for i=0 to 31
	      			point = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		  			par = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i)
					if oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)<>"" then oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)=" "
					oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.parameter(i+1)=""
					if asc(left(point,1))<>32 then
		     			oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)=point
		     			oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.parameter(i+1)=par
						oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PlotVisible(i+1)=true
		  			end if
				next
     		end if
  		end if
	next
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "upd_chart ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'Prompt to select a trend group in case All_TrdSel.sha is not present in the display
sub trdresp
	on error resume next
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oPage=page
		set oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page
		set oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if

	if bCancel=true then
    	window.clearinterval(tid)
		bCancel=false
		bTrdResp=false
		bGroupSelected = false
    	exit sub
	end if
	istatus=oWnd.getresponse3 ( "No trend group selected! Please select one. ", 0, resp)
	if istatus=1 and resp<>"" then
		trdg = csng(resp)
		if err.number = 0 and trdg > 0 then
			set oshape_TrendSup=oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
			oshape_TrendSup.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value = trdg
		else
    		oWnd.messagezonetext "this is not a valid trend number!"
			istatus=0
		end if
		bTrdResp=false
		bGroupSelected = true
    	window.clearinterval (tid)
		call add2trend(sParameter)
	end if
	if istatus=0 then window.clearinterval (tid)
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "trdresp ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'Add the parameter stored in sParameter to the selected trend group
sub add2trend(sparam)
	dim oshape, oshape_c, oshape_sel,cm_point, cm_param, trd_num, empty_place, pointpar, tagn
	on error resume next
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oPage=page
		set oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page
		set oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if

	set oshape_sel=oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("trdsel"))
	selrecoffset=oshape_sel.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	set oshape = page.document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	set oshape_c=oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	Trd_Par= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParam)
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_Par,".")
	sPoint_CP= Mid(Trd_Par,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameter_CP = Mid(Trd_Par, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_point = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPoint_CP)
	if cm_point = "" then cm_point = sPoint_cp
	if instr(cm_point,"{%")>0 then cm_point=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
	cm_param = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameter_CP)
	if cm_param = "" then cm_param = sParameter_CP
	trd_num = oShape_c.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	if isnull(oPage.getattribute("TrdSel")) and bGroupSelected = false then
		sParameter = sparam
		oPage.focus
		bTrdResp=true
		tid = window.setinterval ("trdresp",500)
    	exit sub
	end if
	if oShape_c.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and oWnd.securitylevel<4 then
		oWnd.messagezonetext "The trend group no " & trd_num & " is protected!"
		exit sub
	end if
	exist = 0
	empty_place = -1
	for i=0 to 31
		pointpar = oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		if ucase(pointpar) = ucase (cm_point) and ucase(oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i)) = ucase (cm_param) then exist = 1
		if pointpar=" " and empty_place = -1 then empty_place = i
	next
	if exist = 0 then
		if empty_place >= 0 then
			oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").setvalue empty_place, cm_point
			oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").setvalue empty_place, cm_param
			free = right(oShape_sel.title,2)
			free=free-1
        	oShape_sel.title="Free Pens " & free
			if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Free Pens " & free
			oWnd.messagezonetext ucase(cm_point) & "." & ucase(cm_param) & " Has been added to trend group no " & trd_num
			set oSview=getobject("","Honeywell.Workspace.Client")
			If oSview.IsManagerActive = true then
				reload_trend=false
				reload_popup=false
				for each oWndw in ownd.parent.StationWindows
					recoffset=oWndw.currentrecordoffset
					currpage=oWndw.currentpage
					if lcase(currpage)="systnd03.htm" and cint(recoffset)=cint(selrecoffset) then
					   reload_trend=true
					   set oWindow_Tnd=oWndw
					elseif lcase(currpage)="chart_popup_cp.htm" then
						if cint(selrecoffset)=cint(mid(oWndw.document.title,14)) then
					   		reload_popup=true
					   		set oWindow_Pop=oWndw
						end if
					end if
				next
				if reload_trend=true then
					oSview.setoutputfocuswnd(oWindow_Tnd.AppWindow.HWND)
					window.setTimeout "reloadTrend", 1000
				end if
				if reload_popup=true then
					oSview.setoutputfocuswnd(oWindow_Pop.AppWindow.HWND)
					window.setTimeout "reloadPopup", 1000
				end if
			else
				set chrt_pop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("dummy" & selrecoffset)
				if typename(chrt_pop)<>"Nothing" then
					chrt_pop.close
					window.setTimeout "reloadPopup", 1000
				end if
			end if
		else
			oWnd.messagezonetext "Trend group no " & trd_num & " is full!"
		end if
	else
		oWnd.messagezonetext ucase(cm_point) & "." & ucase(cm_param) & " Has been already configured in trend group no " & trd_num
	end if
	window.settimeout "upd_chart", 1000
	bGroupSelected = false
	if err.number <> 0 then oWnd.LogMessage "add2trend ->" & oShape.id & " " & sparam & " " & err.description
end sub

sub reloadTrend()
	oWindow_Tnd.requestserverlrn 21, 8, selrecoffset
end sub

sub reloadPopup()
	call trd_popup
end sub</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript event=onshapeload for=shape001 defer>Page.SetAttribute "CMenu_GSH",me.id</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=F4A378A5-DDBB-4C15-8638-B570199A27C6 defer
shaperefcount="1">'This shape provides the Solution Pack Context Menu functionality.
'All subroutines are called within SP context menu files

'Acknowledge the alarm on currently selected point
sub acknowledge()
    on error resume next
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	oWnd.invokemenu2 "Acknowledge Alarm"
end sub

'Invoke detail display of currently selected point
sub detail()
	on error resume next
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	oWnd.invokemenu2("Request Point Detail")
end sub

'Invoke the trend group where currently selected point is configured or prompt for trend group number.
sub trd_invoke()
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, custtrd
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	custtrd=oshape.getcustomproperty("num","cp_custtrd")
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	if custtrd<>"" then
		oWnd.requestserverlrn 21,8,custtrd
	else
		oWnd.invokemenu2("request trend")
	end if
end sub

'Invoke the group where currently selected point is configured or prompt for group number.
sub grp_invoke()
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, custgrp
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	custgrp = oshape.getcustomproperty("num","cp_custgrp")
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	if custgrp <> "" then
		oWnd.requestserverlrn 21,49,custgrp
	else
		oWnd.invokemenu2("request group")
	end if
end sub

'Invoke associated display for currently selected point
sub ascdsp()
    on error resume next
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	oWnd.invokemenu2("associated display")
end sub

'Open help file using Windows Explorer, if configured in cm_file custom property
sub help ()
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, spath, cm_file
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_file = oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cm_file")
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	if instr(cm_file,"\")>0 then
 		oWnd.shell "explorer.exe " & cm_file,1
	else
 		spath = page.document.url
 		spath=mid (spath,8,len(spath))
 		spath=mid (spath,1,instrrev(spath,"\"))  & "helpfiles\" & cm_file
 		spath = Replace(spath,"%20"," ")
 		oWnd.shell "explorer.exe " & spath,1
	end if
end sub

'Called in OnPage event of the Help Item of the Context Menu. Manipulates the menu item text.
sub pageload(osource)
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, spath, cm_file
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_label = oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cm_label")
	if (cm_label <> "") then
		oSource.all("text").innertext=" " & cm_label
		cm_file=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cm_file")
		if cm_file="" then osource.disabled=true
	end if
end sub

' Enable/Disable Add Traces to Trend functionality
sub AddTrdCnfg (oSource, sText)
	On Error Resume Next
	dim oShape, spath, cm_param
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oPage=page
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page
	end if
	if isnull(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH")) then
   		oSource.disabled = True
  		exit sub
	end if
	set oShape = document.getElementById(page.getattribute("LastSelection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_param = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sText)
	if cm_param = "" then oSource.disabled = True
end sub

'Enable/Disable View Trend Configuration functionality. Called in OnPage event of View Trend Item
Sub View_Trd_Load(oSource)
	on error resume next
		if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oPage=page
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page
	end if

	if isnull(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH")) or isnull(oPage.getattribute("TrdSel"))  then oSource.disabled = True
    if oSource.id="mnu_View_TrdCnf" then
		for each oObj in oPage.all
	   		if instr(ucase(oObj.classname),"HSC.TREND.1")<>0 then oSource.disabled = True
		next
	end if
end sub

'View Trend Configuration if enabled in View_Trd_Load
sub view_trd
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oPage=page
		set oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page
		set oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if

	set oshape=oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	if oshape.objects("PbHide").style.visibility="visible" then oshape.objects("PbHide").click
	oshape.style.zindex=5000
	iPageWidth = oPage.style.pixelwidth
	iSelectedShapeX = oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode.style.pixelleft
	if iSelectedShapeX > iPageWidth/2 then
		iTableLeft = 20
	else
		iTableLeft = iPageWidth - 420
	end if
	oshape.style.pixelleft=iTableLeft
	oshape.style.pixeltop=80
	oshape.objects("trdtbl").style.visibility="visible"
	oshape.objects("trdtbl").style.pixelwidth=400
	oshape.objects("PbHide").style.visibility="visible"
	oshape.objects("PbHide").style.pixelleft=oshape.objects("PbHide").style.pixelleft+336
	if oShape.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and oWnd.securitylevel<4 then oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=400
end sub

'Invoke mini trend popup
sub view_mini_trend
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape = document.getElementById(page.getattribute("LastSelection")).parentnode.parentnode
	Trd_ParSP= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter","Trd_PointSP")
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_ParSP,".")
	sPointSP_CP= Mid(Trd_ParSP,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameterSP_CP = Mid(Trd_ParSP, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_pointSP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPointSP_CP)
	if cm_pointSP = "" then cm_pointSP = sPointSP_cp
	cm_paramSP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameterSP_CP)
	if cm_paramSP = "" then cm_paramSP = sParameterSP_CP
	Trd_ParPV= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter","Trd_PointPV")
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_ParPV,".")
	sPointPV_CP= Mid(Trd_ParPV,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameterPV_CP = Mid(Trd_ParPV, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_pointPV = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPointPV_CP)
	if cm_pointPV = "" then cm_pointPV = sPointPV_cp
	cm_paramPV = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameterPV_CP)
	if cm_paramPV = "" then cm_paramPV = sParameterPV_CP
    Trd_ParOP= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter","Trd_PointOP")
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_ParOP,".")
	sPointOP_CP= Mid(Trd_ParOP,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameterOP_CP = Mid(Trd_ParOP, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_pointOP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPointOP_CP)
	if cm_pointOP = "" then cm_pointOP = sPointOP_cp
	cm_paramOP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameterOP_CP)
	if cm_paramOP = "" then cm_paramOP = sParameterOP_CP
	xcoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Mini_Trend","left_position")
	ycoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Mini_Trend","top_position")
	set oSview=getobject("","Honeywell.Workspace.Client")
	If oSview.IsManagerActive = false then
		if xcoord="default" or ycoord="default" then
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow "Mini_Trend_Popup.htm?currentpoint=minitrd"& round (rnd*10000) & "&Point1=" & cm_pointPV & "&Point2=" & cm_pointSP & "&Point3=" & cm_pointOP & "&Parameter1=" & cm_paramPV & "&Parameter2=" & cm_paramSP & "&Parameter3="& cm_paramOP, 0, 0, 1
		else
			set oMini_trd=HdxPopupBehavior.FindPopup("minitrd")
			if typename(oMini_trd)<>"Nothing" then oMini_trd.close
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow "Mini_Trend_Popup.htm?currentpoint=minitrd"& round (rnd*10000) & "&Point1=" & cm_pointPV & "&Point2=" & cm_pointSP & "&Point3=" & cm_pointOP & "&Parameter1=" & cm_paramPV & "&Parameter2=" & cm_paramSP & "&Parameter3="& cm_paramOP, xcoord, ycoord, 0
		end if
    else
		hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow "Mini_Trend_Popup.htm?currentpoint=minitrd"& round (rnd*10000) & "&Point1=" & cm_pointPV & "&Point2=" & cm_pointSP & "&Point3=" & cm_pointOP & "&Parameter1=" & cm_paramPV & "&Parameter2=" & cm_paramSP & "&Parameter3="& cm_paramOP, 0, 0, 1
	end if
end sub

' Invoke trend popup. called within sp_systrdsel_sm.xml
sub trd_popup
    on error resume next
	if instr(typename(window.external),"IHDXPopupWindow")=0 then
		set	oPage=page
		set oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page
		set oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	set oShape = oPage.document.getElementById(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	sFP = "Chart_Popup_CP.htm?currentpoint=dummy" & oShape.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	for i = 1 to 32
		point = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i-1)
		par = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i-1)
		sFP = sFP & "&Point" & i & "=" & point & "&Parameter"& i & "=" & par
	next
	xcoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Trend_popup","left_position")
	ycoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Trend_popup","top_position")
	set oSview=getobject("","Honeywell.Workspace.Client")
	If oSview.IsManagerActive = false then
		if xcoord="default" or ycoord="default" then
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow sFP,0,0,1
		else
			set oChrt_Popup=HdxPopupBehavior.FindPopup("dummy")
			if typename(oChrt_Popup)<>"Nothing" then oChrt_Popup.close
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow sFP,xcoord,ycoord,0
		end if
    else
		hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow sFP,0,0,1
	end if
end sub

'Enable/Disable Detail Display Functionality in Fire and Gas Context Menu.
sub fire_gas_pageload(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	index = cint(right(oSource.id,1) )
	mn_txt=oShape.getcustomproperty("Point",oShape.customproperties.Item(index).name)
	if mn_txt="" then
   		oSource.disabled=true
	else
  		oSource.all("text").innertext="Detail " & ucase(mn_txt)
   		oSource.disabled=false
	end if
	if err.number <>0 then window.external.LogMessage "fire_gas_pageload ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description
end sub

'Invoke Detail Display for Fire and Gas point if enabled in fire_gas_pageload
sub fire_gas_detail(oSource)
    on error resume next
	window.external.requesttask 21,73,0,0,0,mid(oSource.all("text").innertext,8,len(oSource.all("text").innertext)-7)
end sub</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=E62C0461-A02B-4285-9508-FC76B85214D9 defer
shaperefcount="1">'--------------------------------------
'RegCntl - Mode; EUDesc;
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_RegCntl_Mode_EUDesc_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim sMode, iPID_HialmT, iDac_HialmT, iAlmEnbState, iRedTag, oShape,oPIDAlm,oDACAlm
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
      sMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_mode")
	  oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(sMode,1)
	  if instr(sMode,"#")<>0 then oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=""
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if sMode=sNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
   if instr(oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value,"N")>0 then
   		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").currentvalue="--------"
   		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").textcolor=vbred
   else
   		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass
   end if
   if err.number <>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_RegCntl_Mode_EUDesc_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Numeric Values
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim sFormat,oShape, nNumericFormat
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomProperty("Parameter","cp_pvformat")<>"" then
		nNumericFormat=oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_pvformat")
   else
   		nNumericFormat=oShape.getcustomProperty("Text","cp_pvformat")
   end if
   if IsNumeric(nNumericFormat) and instr(nNumericFormat,".")=0 then
  		sFormat="%." & nNumericFormat & "f"
  		if typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
   else
   		window.external.LogMessage "CDA_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & "Invalid PV Format: " & nNumericFormat
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Graph objects
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_ExecState_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oExStObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oExStObj=oShape.objects("LinExecState")
   if oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_execstate")=1 then
      oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
   else
	  oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_ExecState_Alp_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Graph objects
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_ExecState_1_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oExStObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oExStObj=oShape.objects("LinExecState")
   set oBad=oShape.objects("RecFrame")
   if oSource.value=1 then
      oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
   else
	  oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
   end if
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
   		oBad.styleclass="RecPV_" & oShape.getcustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
   else
		oBad.styleclass="RecFrame"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_ExecState_1_Alp_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'OnOffValves
'-------------------------------------------------------
sub CDA_OnOffValves_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim igPV,oShape,igOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   igPV=oSource.value
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then igPV="Bad"
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
   oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if cstr(igPV)="Bad" then
   	  iBad="B"
   elseif igPV=0 then
      iBad="B"
   end if
   blf=false
   if iBad="B" then
     oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
	 if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass = "WdgESD_" & index
   else
     if oShape.getCustomProperty("Point","tagnameLF")<>"" then
		if oSource.datavalue("tagnameLF.cp_pvLF")=cint(oshape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_LFActive")) then
		   bLF=true
		   if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then
		   		oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass="WdgESD_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV0")
		   		oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_Regvalve"
		   elseif typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then
		   		oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV0")
           end if
		end if
	 end if
	 if not bLF then
	 	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_ESDValue")<>"" then
		   iESDval=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_ESDValue")
		   sFState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_FailState")
		   if oSource.datavalue("tagnameDO.cp_opDO")=cint(iESDval) then
			  if sFState="FC" then: iGOP=4 :else: iGOP=5: end if
		   else
		      if sFState="FC" then: iGOP=5 :else: iGOP=4: end if
		   end if
		   if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then
		   	  oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass="WdgESD_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iGOP)
			  oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_Regvalve"
		   elseif typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then
		   	  oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iGOP)
           end if
	    end if
	 end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_OnOffValves_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_OnOffValvesC_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim igPV,oShape,igOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then: igPV="Bad": else: igPV=oSource.value: end if
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
   oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if cstr(igPV)="Bad" then
   	  iBad="B"
   elseif igPV=0 then
      iBad="B"
   end if
   if iBad="B" then
        if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
   else
   		if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_gop")<>"" then
   			igOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_gop")
			indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igOP)
			if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & indexOP
        else
			oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_OnOffValve_OPNA"
   		end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_OnOffValvesC_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'MOV
'-------------------------------------------------------
sub CDA_MOV_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim igPV,oShape,igOP,index,indexOP
   'on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   igPV=oSource.value
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then igPV="Bad"
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
   oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if cstr(igPV)="Bad" then
   	  iBad="B"
   elseif igPV=0 then
      iBad="B"
   end if
   if iBad="B" then
       oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
       oShape.objects("PlgC").style.visibility = "hidden"
	   oShape.objects("PlgO").style.visibility = "hidden"
   else
   	   if oShape.getcustomProperty("Parameter","cp_ts")<>"" then
			iTorque=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_ts")
			sStyleTrq=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForTorque" & iTorque)
			oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & sStyleTrq
   	   else
	        oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_MOV"
       end if
	   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_gop")<>"" then
   		   igOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_gop")
		   indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igOP)
           oShape.objects("PlgO").styleclass="PlgO_" & indexOP
		   oShape.objects("PlgC").styleclass="PlgC_" & indexOP
		   if index=IndexOP then
		       oShape.objects("PlgC").style.visibility = "hidden"
		       oShape.objects("PlgO").style.visibility = "hidden"
           end if
	   end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_MOV_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_MOVInd_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,rVal
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.value
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_gpv")<>"" then rVal=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
'   if instr(ucase(rVal),"N")=0 then
'   		if rVal>100 then
'      		rVal=100
'   		elseif rVal<0 then
'      		rVal=0
'   		end if
'   end if
   oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(rVal) & "%"
   oShape.objects("TxtOP").styleclass="ConAlpOP"
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad and instr(rVal,"N")=0 then
      oShape.objects("TxtOP").styleclass="ConAlpOP_Bad"
   elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad or instr(rVal,"N")<>0 then
      oShape.objects("TxtOP").value="------"
      oShape.objects("TxtOP").textcolor=vbred
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_MOVInd_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'Regulatory Control Valves
'-------------------------------------------------------

sub CDA_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim rVal,rClosed,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.value
   rClosed=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","ClosedValue")
   rOpen=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","OpenValue")
   if rClosed<>"" then rClosed=csng(rClosed)
   if rOpen<>"" then rOpen=csng(rOpen)
'   if rVal>100 then
'      rVal=100
'   elseif rVal<0 then
'      rVal=0
'   end if
   oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(rVal) & "%"
   if rClosed<>"" and rOpen<>"" then
   	if rVal > rClosed and rVal < rOpen then
      	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForInbet" )
   	elseif rVal<=rClosed then
	  	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForClose" )
   	else
	  	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOpen" )
   	end if
   end if

   if (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad and instr(oSource.value,"N")=0 then
		index="RegValve_Bad"
		oShape.objects("TxtOP").styleclass="ConAlpOP_Bad"
		indWdgC=index
   elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood and oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")=1 then
		index="RegValve_BadPV"
		 if instr(ucase(oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_op")),"OP")=0 then
			oShape.objects("TxtOP").styleclass="ConAlpOP"
			oShape.objects("TxtOP").value="------"
			oShape.objects("TxtOP").textcolor=vbred
			indWdgC=index
         else
			indWdgC=index
		 end if
   elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad and instr(oSource.value,"N")<>0 then
		index="RegValve_Bad"
		if instr(ucase(oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_op")),"OP")=0 then
			oShape.objects("TxtOP").styleclass="ConAlpOP"
			oShape.objects("TxtOP").value="------"
			oShape.objects("TxtOP").textcolor=vbred
			indWdgC=index
         else
			indWdgC=index
		 end if
   else
   		oShape.objects("TxtOP").styleclass="ConAlpOP"
		indWdgC="RegValve"
   end if
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & indWdgC
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub


sub CDA_RegValves_AlpGPVGOP_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim igPV,oShape,igOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   igPV=oSource.value
   sClosed=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","ClosedValue")
   if sClosed<>"" then fClosed=csng(sClosed)
   sOpen=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","OpenValue")
   if sOpen<>"" then fOpen=csng(sOpen)
   if sOpen<>"" and sClosed<>"" then
	   iOP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_op")
	   stxt4=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_text4")
	   stxt5=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_text5")
	   if instr(stxt4,"ДВИЖЕНИЕ")>0 and iGPV=4 then
	   		if iOP<=fClosed then iGPV=4 else igPV=1
       elseif instr(stxt5,"ДВИЖЕНИЕ")>0 and iGPV=5 then
			if iOP>=fOpen then iGPV=5 else igPV=1
       else
			igPV=oSource.value
       end if
   end if
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then igPV="Bad"
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if cstr(igPV)="Bad" then
   	  iBad="B"
   elseif igPV=0 then
      iBad="B"
   end if
    if iBad="B" then
     if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
	 if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass = "WdgESD_" & index
   else

	 if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then
	      if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_gop")<>"" then
		  	  iGOP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_gop")
          elseif oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_sifl")<>"" then
		      iSIFL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_sifl")
			  sFState=oShape.getCustomProperty("Text","cp_FailState")
			  if iSIFL=1 then
					  if sFState="FC" then: iGOP=4 :else: iGOP=5: end if
				   else
					  if sFState="FC" then: iGOP=5 :else: iGOP=4: end if
              end if
		   end if
		   oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass="WdgESD_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iGOP)
		   if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_Regvalve"
     end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_RegValves_AlpGPVGOP_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_Damper_AlpVal_OnUpdate(oSource)
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if instr(oShape.src,"poai")=0 then
	   rVal=oSource.value
	   rClosed=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","ClosedValue")
	   rOpen=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","OpenValue")
	   if rClosed<>"" then rClosed=csng(rClosed)
	   if rOpen<>"" then rOpen=csng(rOpen)
'	   if rVal>100 then
'	      rVal=100
'	   elseif rVal<0 then
'	      rVal=0
'	   end if
	   if rClosed<>"" and rOpen<>"" then
		   	if rVal > rClosed and rVal < rOpen then
		      	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForInbet" )
		   	elseif rVal<=rClosed then
			  	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForClose" )
		   	else
			  	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOpen" )
		   	end if
	   end if
		if (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad and instr(oSource.value,"N")=0 then
			index="RegValve_Bad"
		elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood and oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")=1 then
			index="RegValve_BadPV"
	    elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad and instr(oSource.value,"N")<>0 then
			index="RegValve_Bad"
	    end if
	   oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
   else
   		rVal=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_op")
'		if rVal>100 then
'	      rVal=100
'	    elseif rVal<0 then
'	      rVal=0
'	    end if
   end if
   rval=-round(rval)*0.9
   if rVal<-90 then
      rVal=-90
    elseif rVal>0 then
      rVal=0
    end if
   set oLinDamper=oShape.objects("LinDamper")
   oLinDamper.style.visibility="hidden"
   oLinDamper.rotation=rval
   oLinDamper.style.visibility="inherit"
 end sub


'--------------------------------------------
' Pumps
'--------------------------------------------
sub CDA_PumpsM_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim igPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then igPV="Bad": else: igPV=oSource.value
   if cstr(igPV)="Bad" then
   	  iBad="B"
   elseif igPV=0 then
      iBad="B"
   end if

   if oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_healthy")<>"" then
        iHealthy=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_healthy")
   		if iHealthy = 1 then
			iHealthy="PV" & igPV
            if iGPV=6 then iHealthy="PV5"
	    else
			iHealthy="NotHealthy"
		end if
   end if
   if iBad<>"B" and typename(oShape.objects("OvlBodyH")) <> "Nothing" then
        index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleFor" & iHealthy)
		if iHealthy="NotHealthy" then: sName="OvlBodyH_": else: sName="OvlBody_": end if
		oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass=sName & index
   else
        index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   	    oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass="OvlBodyH_" & index
   end if
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index

   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_speed")="2" and typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then
	   iSpeed=iGPV-4
	   if iSpeed>0 then
	        if iGPV=6 then iGPV=5
            index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
	        if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
            if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
            if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
	   		oShape.objects("TxtM").value=iSpeed
	   else
	    	oShape.objects("TxtM").value="M"
	   end if
   elseif oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_speed")="-" and typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then
		oShape.objects("TxtM").value=""
   end if

   if oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_dol")<>"" and typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then
   		idol=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_dol")
		if iDol=1 then
        	oShape.objects("TxtM").value=oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_doltxt")
			oShape.objects("TxtM").styleclass="TxtMDOL"
		else
		    oShape.objects("TxtM").value="M"
		    oShape.objects("TxtM").styleclass="TxtM"
        end if
   end if

   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_modeattr")<>"" then
      iMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_modeattr")
	  if iMode=1 then
	  	oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_mdattrtxt"),1)
	  else
	    oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=right(oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_mdattrtxt"),1)
	  end if
	  if instr(sMode,"#")<>0 then oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=""
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmodeattr")<>"" then
      iNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmodeattr")
	  if iMode=iNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_PumpsM_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

   dim igPV,oShape

   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Motors_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_DigStateCColor_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode

   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & index
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_DigStateCColor_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
