sub CDA_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.breakpointvalueenum
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index
   if instr(oSource.value,"1.#QNAN")=0 and (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
   		sBad=oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & sBad
		oSource.getstyleproperty("hw-fill-color")
		oSource.style.backgroundcolor=oSource.getstyleproperty("hw-fill-color")
		oSource.style.color=oSource.getstyleproperty("hw-text-color")
   else
   		oSource.styleclass=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_DigState4_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=0
   for i=0 to 3
   		if oSource.datavalue("tagname.flag_" & i & ".pvfl")=1 then
			iPV=i
			exit for
		end if
   next
   inNulls=0
   for i=0 to 3
   		if oSource.datavalue("tagname.flag_" & i & ".pvfl")=0 then
			inNulls=inNulls+1
		end if
   next

   if inNulls=4 then
   	oShape.objects("TxtPV").innertext=oShape.objects("TxtPV").innertext
   else
    oShape.objects("TxtPV").innertext=oSource.datavalue("tagname.flag_" & iPV & ".statetext[1]")
   end if
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   oShape.objects("TxtPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index
   if instr(oSource.value,"NAN")>0 or (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
	   if instr(oSource.value,"N")>0 then
	   		oShape.objects("txtPV").value="--------"
	   		oShape.objects("txtPV").textcolor=vbred
	   else
   	   		sBad=oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
	   		oShape.objects("TxtPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & sBad
	   end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_DigState4_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_DigState5_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource,nSts)
   dim oShape, sName(7)
   'on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=0
   for i=0 to nSts
   		sName(i)=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_name_flag" & i)
		sNm="tagname." & sName(i) & ".pvfl"
		if oSource.datavalue(sNm)=1 then
			iPV=i
			exit for
		end if
   next
   inNulls=0
   for i=0 to nSts
   		sName(i)=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_name_flag" & i)
		sNm="tagname." & sName(i) & ".pvfl"
   		if oSource.datavalue(sNm)=0 then
			inNulls=inNulls+1
		end if
   next
   if inNulls=nSts+1 then
   		oShape.objects("TxtPV").innertext=oShape.objects("TxtPV").innertext
   else
   		sNm=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_name_flag" & iPV)
   		sNm="tagname." & sNm & ".statetext[1]"
    	oShape.objects("TxtPV").innertext=oSource.datavalue(sNm)
   end if
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   oShape.objects("TxtPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index
   if instr(oSource.value,"NAN")>0 or (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
	   if instr(oSource.value,"N")>0 then
	   		oShape.objects("txtPV").value="--------"
	   		oShape.objects("txtPV").textcolor=vbred
	   else
   	   		sBad=oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
	   		oShape.objects("TxtPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & sBad
	   end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_DigState4_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'---------------------------------------------------
'SCMs
'---------------------------------------------------

sub Scm_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim hnd, stp, state,p
	on error resume next
	strip=1
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if strip=1 then
   		hnd=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_handler")
   		p=instrrev(hnd,".")
   		hnd=mid(hnd,p+1,len(hnd)-p)
   		oShape.objects("TxtHandler").innertext=hnd
	elseif strip=2 then
		hnd=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_handler")
   		p=instrrev(hnd,".")
   		hndh=mid(hnd,p+1,len(hnd)-p)
   		hndn=left(hnd,p)
   		oShape.objects("TxtHandler").innertext=hndn & vbCRLF & hndh
	else
   		oShape.objects("TxtHandler").innertext=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_handler")
	end if
	stp=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_step")
	p=instrrev(stp,".")
	stp=mid(stp,p+1,len(stp)-p)
	oShape.objects("TxtStep").innertext=stp
	state=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_state")
	select case state
		case 0  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="Loading"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 1  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="LOADED"
		case 2  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="INACTIVE"
		case 3  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="VALIDATED"
		case 4  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="RUNNING"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 5  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="COMPLETE"
		case 6  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="CHECKING"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 7  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="IDLE"
		case 8  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="INTERRUPTING"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 9  oShape.objects("TxtState").value="INTERRUPTED"
		case 10 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="RESTARTING"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 11 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="RESTARTED"
		case 12 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="HOLDING"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 13 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="HELD"
		case 14 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="STOPPING"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 15 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="STOPPED"
		case 16 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="ABORTING"
        		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility="inherit"
		case 17 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="ABORTED"
		case 18 oShape.objects("TxtState").value="COMERR"
	end select
	iPer=oSource.datavalue("tagname.period")
	select case iPer
		case 0 iPeriod=1
		case 1 iPeriod=0.005
		case 2 iPeriod=0.01
		case 3 iPeriod=0.02
		case 4 iPeriod=0.05
		case 5 iPeriod=0.1
		case 6 iPeriod=0.2
		case 7 iPeriod=0.5
		case 8 iPeriod=1
		case 9 iPeriod=2
	end select
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("TxtState").value=""
		oShape.objects("TxtHandler").value=""
		oShape.objects("TxtStep").value=""
    else
		oSource.innertext=oSource.value*iPeriod
	end if

end sub</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=CEC55C18-38FE-411B-B7F4-3EA6FD82AE99 defer
shaperefcount="1">Dim iBit0, iBit1, iBit2, iBit3, iBit4, iBit5, iBit6, iBit7, iBit8, iBit9, iBit10, iBit11, iBit12, iBit13, iBit14, iBit15, iBit16

	iBit15=32768: iBit14=16384: iBit13=8192: iBit12=4096: iBit11=2048: iBit10=1024: iBit9=512: iBit8=256
	iBit7=128: iBit6=64: Bit5=32: iBit4=16: iBit3=8: iBit2=4: iBit1=2: iBit0=1


'MOS and OOS shapes in process displays
'***********************************************************************************************************************************************


sub Scd_MOS_OOS_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape, iPV
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iValueON = csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_ONValue"))
    iPV=100
	if instr(oShape.src,"_02_")>0 then
    	iPV2=oSource.datavalue("Tagname2.cp_pv")
	    if iPV2 = iValueON then: iPV = 1: else: iPV = 0: end if
		if (oSource.dataquality("Tagname2.cp_pv") and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then iPV = 2
	end if
    if iPV=100 or iPV=0 then
		if oSource.value = iValueON then: iPV = 1: else: iPV = 0: end if
		if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then iPV = 2
	end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
		iMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
		if iMode=1 and iPV<>2 then oShape.objects("RecMode").style.visibility="inherit" else oShape.objects("RecMode").style.visibility="hidden"
	end if
	index = oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_Style" & iPV)
	if typename(oShape.objects("TxtMOS"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("TxtMOS").styleclass = "TxtMOS_" & index
	if typename(oShape.objects("TxtOOS"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("TxtOOS").styleclass = "TxtOOS_" & index
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_MOS_OOS_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------------
' Pumps
'--------------------------------------------
sub Scd_PumpsM_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
   		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
		index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV0")
   		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBodyH")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass=oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass
   elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
   		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
   		iPV=oSource.value
   		index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
		ibadstate=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badstate"):if ibadstate="" then ibadstate=0
		ibad=ibadstate
   		if oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameBAD")<>"" then
        	ibad=oSource.datavalue("tagnameBAD.cp_pvbad")
   			if cint(ibad)=cint(ibadstate) and typename(oShape.objects("OvlBodyH")) <> "Nothing" then
				oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass="OvlBodyH_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForFault")
            else
				oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass=oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass
			end if
   		end if
   else
        index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForBAD")
   		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBodyH")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass=oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_PumpsM_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub Scd_PumpsM1_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
   		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
		index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV0")
   		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBodyH")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass=oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass
   elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
   		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
   		iPV=oSource.value
   		index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBodyH")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass=oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass
		if oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameRTS")<>"" then
        	iRTS=oSource.datavalue("tagnameRTS.cp_pv")
   			if iRTS=0 then oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass="OvlBodyH_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForNotHealthy")
   		end if
   else
        index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForBAD")
   		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   		if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBodyH")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBodyH").styleclass=oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
   end if

   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
      iMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_mode")
	  if iMode=0 then
	  	oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_mdtxt"),1)
	  else
	    oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=right(oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_mdtxt"),1)
	  end if
	  if instr(sMode,"#")<>0 then oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=""
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      iNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if iMode=iNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_PumpsM1_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'------------------------------------------
'Digital States
'------------------------------------------
sub Scd_DigStateOnScanQual(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
	else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecFrame"
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_DigStateOnScanQual ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub Scd_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape,oPVObj
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iBadStatePV=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badstate")
	if iBadStatePV="" then iBadStatePV=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_badstate")
	iBad=100
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameBAD")<>"" then iBad=oSource.datavalue("tagnameBAD.cp_pvbad")
	set oPVObj=oShape.objects("TxtPV")
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
  		oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
  		oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityBadComm)=iQualityBadComm then
  		oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Bad"
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
    elseif cint(ibad)=cint(iBadStatePV) then
        oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Bad_LG"
        oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	    oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
	else
  		oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
  		oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub Scd_DigStateCColor_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iPV=oSource.value
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & index
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
  	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
  		sBad=oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
		oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & sBad
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
    else
   		oSource.styleclass=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
    end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_DigStateCColor_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub Scd_DigStateCColorSPM_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape
	'on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iSysState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_sysstate"):if iSysState="" then iSysState=0
    iBMSState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_bmsstate"):if iBMSState="" then iBMSState=0
    iChnState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_chnstate"):if iChnState="" then iChnState=0
	iSYS=iSysState
	sysUnack=false
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameSYS")<>"" then
		iSYS=oSource.datavalue("tagnameSYS.cp_pvsys")
		sysUnack=(oSource.dataalarm("tagnameSYS.cp_pvsys") and 1024)=1024
    end if
	iBMS=iBMSState
    bmsUnack=false
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameBMS")<>"" then
		iBMS=oSource.datavalue("tagnameBMS.cp_pvbms")
		bmsUnack=(oSource.dataalarm("tagnameBMS.cp_pvbms") and 1024)=1024
    end if
	iChn=iChnState
    chnUnack=false
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameCHN")<>"" then
		iChn=oSource.datavalue("tagnameCHN.cp_pvchn")
		chnUnack=(oSource.dataalarm("tagnameCHN.cp_pvchn") and 1024)=1024
    end if
    iPV=oSource.value

    if oShape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_bit")<>"" then
			nBit=cint(oShape.getCustomProperty("Parameter","cp_bit"))
			s="(iPV and iBit" & nbit & ")/iBit" & nbit
			iPV=eval(s)
	end if

    iPVUnack=(oSource.alarm and 1024)=1024
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
    oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & index
	oShape.objects("RecPV").fillcolorblink=false
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
    elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityBadComm)=iQualityBadComm then
  		sBad=oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
		oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & sBad
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
    elseif cint(iSysState)<>cint(iSYS) then
		oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForSysPV" & iSys)
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
    elseif cint(iBMSState)<>cint(iBMS) then
		oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForBMSPV" & iBMS)
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
    elseif cint(iChnState)<>cint(iChn) then
		oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForChnPV" & iChn)
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
    if iPVUnack=true or sysUnack=true or bmsUnack=true or chnUnack=true then
		oShape.objects("RecPV").fillcolorblink=true
    end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_DigStateCColorSPM_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub Scd_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iBad=100
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameBAD")<>"" then iBad=oSource.datavalue("tagnameBAD.cp_pvbad")
	iBadStatePV=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_badstate")
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_File")="" then
		iPV=oSource.breakpointvalueenum
		index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
		oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index
    else
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV"
	end if
	if instr(oSource.value,"1.#QNAN")=0 and (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood or cint(ibad)=cint(iBadStatePV) then
	  if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)<>iQualityOffScan or cint(ibad)=cint(iBadStatePV) then
   			sBad=oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
			oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & sBad
			oSource.getstyleproperty("hw-fill-color")
			oSource.style.backgroundcolor=oSource.getstyleproperty("hw-fill-color")
			oSource.style.color=oSource.getstyleproperty("hw-text-color")
      end if
    end if
	if instr(oShape.src,"PU_")>0 then oShape.objects("TxtName").value=oSource.datavalue("tagname.name")
    if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub Scd_Burner_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if ((oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan) or ((oSource.dataquality("TagnameB.cp_pv") and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan) then
	   oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
       iPV="0"
	elseif ((oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood) or ((oSource.dataquality("TagnameB.cp_pv") and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood) then
	   oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	   iPV="Bad"
	else
	   oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	   iPVm=oSource.value
	   iPVb=oSource.datavalue("TagnameB.cp_pv")
	   iPV=cstr(iPVm*2+iPVb)
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("RecBrnrPV").styleclass="RecBrnrPV_" & index
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_Burner_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub Scd_BrnrCTxt_AlpOnupdate(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
    iPV=cstr(oSource.value)
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecFrame_" & index
	oShape.objects("TxtCtext").styleclass="TxtCtext_" & index
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_BrnrCTxt_AlpOnupdate(oSource) ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub


'--------------------------------------
'Numeric Values
'--------------------------------------
sub Scd_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim sFormat,oShape, nNumericFormat
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	nNumericFormat = oshape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_PVFormat")
	if isnumeric(trim(nNumericFormat)) then
		sFormat="%." & trim(nNumericFormat) & "f"
		if typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
		if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlpSP"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
	else
		window.external.LogMessage "Scd_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & "Invalid PV Format: " & nNumericFormat
	end if
    iBadStatePV=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badstate")
	iBad=100
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","tagnameBAD")<>"" then iBad=oSource.datavalue("TagnameBad.cp_pvBad")

   	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffscan)=iQualityOffscan then
   	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityBadComm)=iQualityBadComm then
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Bad"
		oSource.style.backgroundcolor=oSource.fillcolor
    elseif cint(ibad)=cint(iBadStatePV)  then
	 	if instr(ucase(oShape.src),"PU")>0 then
			oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV"
			oSource.textcolor=vbred
			oSource.innertext="--------"
		else
			oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Bad_LG"
        end if
	else
	    oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV"
    end if
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffscan)<>iQualityOffscan then
	   oSource.style.backgroundcolor=oSource.fillcolor
       oSource.style.color=oSource.textcolor
    end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
    if oSource.dataexists("tagname.name") then oShape.objects("TxtName").value=oSource.datavalue("tagname.name")
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'*******************************************************************************************************************************
sub Scd_Numeric_AlpPVFormatTGS_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim sFormat,oShape, nNumericFormat
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	nNumericFormat = oshape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_PVFormat")
	if isnumeric(trim(nNumericFormat)) then
		sFormat="%." & trim(nNumericFormat) & "f"
		if typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
		if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlpSP"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
	else
		window.external.LogMessage "Scd_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & "Invalid PV Format: " & nNumericFormat
	end if

	iCIUState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_ciustate"):if iCIUState="" then iCIUState=0
    iTNKState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_tnkstate"):if iTNKState="" then iTNKState=0
    iChnState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_chnstate"):if iChnState="" then iChnState=0
	iTXState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_txstate"):if iTXState="" then iTXState=0

	iCIU=iCIUState:	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameCIU")<>"" then iCIU=oSource.datavalue("tagnameCIU.cp_pvciu")
	iTNK=iTNKState:	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameTank")<>"" then iTNK=oSource.datavalue("tagnametank.cp_pvtank")
	iChn=iChnState:	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameBAD")<>"" then 	iChn=oSource.datavalue("tagnameBAD.cp_pvbad")
	iTX=iTXState:	if oshape.getcustomproperty("Point","TagnameCHN")<>"" then 	iTX=oSource.datavalue("tagnameCHN.cp_pvchn")

    if (oSource.quality and iQualityBadComm)=iQualityBadComm then
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Bad"
		oSource.style.backgroundcolor=oSource.fillcolor
		oSource.style.color=oSource.textcolor
    elseif cint(iCIUState)<>cint(iCIU) then
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForCIUPV" & iCIU)
    elseif cint(iTNKState)<>cint(iTNK) then
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForTNKPV" & iTNK)
    elseif cint(iTXState)<>cint(iTX) then
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForTXPV" & iTX)
    elseif cint(iChnState)<>cint(iChn) then
		oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForChnPV" & iChn)
    else
	    oSource.styleclass="ScConAlpPV"
	end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
    if oSource.dataexists("tagname.name") then oShape.objects("TxtName").value=oSource.datavalue("tagname.name")
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_Numeric_AlpPVFormatTGS_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub


'----------------------------------------
'Deviation Alarm
'----------------------------------------
sub Scd_Dev_Alarm_Icon(oSource,iTagN)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iDev=0
	iAllUnack=0
	for i=1 to iTagN
		iDev=iDev+oSource.datavalue ("AlmTag" & i & ".cp_DevL" & i)
		iDev=iDev+oSource.datavalue ("AlmTag" & i & ".cp_DevH" & i)
		iUnack=oSource.dataAlarm ("AlmTag" & i & ".cp_DevL" & i)
		if (iUnack and 1024)=1024 then iAllUnack=iAllUnack+1
	next
	if iDev>0 then
		oShape.objects("AlarmState").priority=1
		if iAllUnack>0 then
		    oShape.objects("AlarmState").Acknowledged=0
        else
			oShape.objects("AlarmState").Acknowledged=1
        end if
     else
		oShape.objects("AlarmState").priority=0
	 end if
	 if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_Dev_Alarm_Icon ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'----------------------------------------
'Cause and Effect
'----------------------------------------

sub Scd_CEXXX_Click
    dim oShape
	set oShape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("ESD_matrix"))
	oShape.objects("RecTarg").click
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_CEXXX_Click ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description
end sub


Sub Scd_CEXXX_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iPV=oSource.value
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then iPV=2
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
		iMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
		if iMode=1 and iPV<>2 then oShape.objects("RecMode").style.visibility="inherit" else oShape.objects("RecMode").style.visibility="hidden"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & index
	if instr(lcase(oShape.src),"scd_cetp_lib_h_pai_") then
		N=cint(left(right(oShape.src,11),2))
		for i=1 to N
			oShape.objects("TxtCText" & i).styleclass="TxtCText_" & index
		Next
	else
		oShape.objects("TxtCText").styleclass="TxtCText_" & index
	end if
	if typename(oShape.objects("TxtPName"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("TxtPName").styleclass="TxtCText_" & index
	if iPV<2 then
	   oShape.objects("RecPV").alarm=oSource.alarm
    else
	   oShape.objects("RecPV").alarm=2048
    end if
	if typename(oShape.objects("TxtAnValue"))<>"Nothing" then
		iAType=cint(right(oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_tp"),1))
		select case iAType
			case 1: sLim="HH"
			case 2: sLim="H"
			case 3: sLim="L"
			case 4: sLim="LL"
		end select
		sEuDesc=oSource.datavalue("tagnameA.cp_eudesc")
		iPVFrmt=oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat")
		fAnValue=oSource.datavalue("tagnameA.cp_pv")
		fTP=oSource.datavalue("tagnameA.cp_tp")
		oShape.objects("TxtAnValue").value=FormatNumber(fAnValue,iPVFrmt) & " " & sEuDesc & "    " & sLim & " " & FormatNumber(fTP,iPVFrmt)
		oShape.objects("TxtAnValue").styleclass="TxtCText_" & index
	end if

	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
    else
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_Active"
    end if

	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_CEXXX_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'----------------------------------------
'OnOff Valves
'----------------------------------------

sub Scd_OnOffValves_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iPV=oSource.value
	iOP=10
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then iOP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_op")
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
	else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if

	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
	oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
	if cstr(iPV)="Bad" then
		iBad="B"
	elseif iPV=3 then
		iBad="B"
	end if

	blf=false
	if iBad="B" then
		oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
	else
		if oShape.getCustomProperty("Point","tagnameLF")<>"" then
			if oSource.datavalue("tagnameLF.cp_pvLF")=cint(oshape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_LFActive")) then
				bLF=true
				if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then
					oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV3")
				end if
			end if
		end if
		if not bLF then
			if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" and iOP<>10 then
				oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOP" & iOP)
			elseif typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" and iOP=10 then
				oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_OnOffValve_OPNA"
			end if
		end if
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " SCD_OnOffValves_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub Scd_OnOffValves_Deluge_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode

	iCmd=oSource.datavalue("tagnameCMD.cp_pv")
	iPV=oSource.datavalue("TagnameP.cp_pv")
	iOP=oSource.value*2
	iMOS=oSource.datavalue("TagnameMOS.cp_pv")

	if iCmd=0 then iPV=3
	if iMOS=1 or iPV=1 then IPV=0

	if ((oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan) or ((oSource.dataquality("tagnameCMD.cp_pv") and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan) or ((oSource.dataquality("tagnameP.cp_pv") and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan) or ((oSource.dataquality("tagnameMOS.cp_pv") and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan)then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif ((oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood) or ((oSource.dataquality("tagnameCMD.cp_pv") and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood) or ((oSource.dataquality("tagnameP.cp_pv") and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood) or ((oSource.dataquality("tagnameMOS.cp_pv") and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood) then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
		iOP="Bad"
	else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
	oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)
	oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " SCD_OnOffValves_Deluge_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub
'----------------------------------------
'Reg Valves
'----------------------------------------

sub Scd_RegValves_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
    if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
	else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
	oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
	if cstr(iPV)="Bad" then
		iBad="B"
	elseif iPV=3 then
		iBad="B"
	end if
   blf=false
   if iBad="B" then
     oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
	 if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass = "WdgESD_" & index
   else
     if oShape.getCustomProperty("Point","tagnameLF")<>"" then
		if oSource.datavalue("tagnameLF.cp_pvLF")=cint(oshape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_LFActive")) then
		   bLF=true
		   if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then
		   		oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass="WdgESD_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV0")
		   		oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_Regvalve"
		   elseif typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then
		   		oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV0")
           end if
		end if
	 end if
	 if not bLF then
	 	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_ESDValue")<>"" then
		   iESDval=oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_ESDValue")
		   sFState=oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_FailState")
		   if oSource.datavalue("tagnameDO.cp_opDO")=cint(iESDval) then
			  if sFState="FC" then: iOP=1 :else: iOP=2: end if
		   else
		      if sFState="FC" then: iOP=2 :else: iOP=1: end if
		   end if
		   if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" then
		   	  oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass="WdgESD_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)
			  oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_Regvalve"
		   elseif typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then
		   	  oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)
           end if
	    end if
	 end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_OnOffValves_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub


'----------------------------------------
'MOValves
'----------------------------------------

sub Scd_MOV_FG_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
dim iPV,oShape,index
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iPV=oSource.value
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
    else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
    oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
    oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
	oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_MOV"
	if iPV="Bad" then
       oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
       oShape.objects("PlgC").style.visibility = "hidden"
	   oShape.objects("PlgO").style.visibility = "hidden"
    else
	   if oShape.getcustomProperty("Point","TagnameTrq")<>"" then
			iTorque=oSource.datavalue("TagnameTrq.cp_pv")
			sStyleTrq=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForTorque" & iTorque)
			oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & sStyleTrq
   	   else
	        oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_MOV"
       end if

	   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		   iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
		   indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOP" & iOP)
           oShape.objects("PlgO").styleclass="PlgO_" & indexOP
		   oShape.objects("PlgC").styleclass="PlgC_" & indexOP
		   if indexOP=index then
		   		oShape.objects("PlgC").style.visibility = "hidden"
	   			oShape.objects("PlgO").style.visibility = "hidden"
           end if
	   end if
    end if

	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_MOV_FG_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'----------------------------------------
'Fire and Gas
'----------------------------------------

Sub Scd_HornBeacon_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape,iQuality(2)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iQuality(0)=oSource.quality
	iQuality(1)=oSource.dataquality("TagnameB.cp_pv")
	for j=0 to 1
		ipQuality = 4
		ipQuality = iQuality(j)
		if (ipQuality and 8)=8 then
      		sOffScan="Active"
			elseif (ipQuality and 4)=4 then
      		sBad="Bad"
    	end if
	next
	if sOffScan="Active" then
	  	oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif sBad="Bad"then
	    oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
  		oShape.objects("DetBody").styleclass="DetBody_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
		oShape.objects("WdgBeac").styleclass="WdgBeac_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
	else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV=oSource.value
		iPVbc=oSource.datavalue("TagnameB.cp_pv")
		index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
		indexbc=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPVbc)
		oShape.objects("DetBody").styleclass="DetBody_" & index
		oShape.objects("WdgBeac").styleclass="WdgBeac_" & indexbc
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_HornBeacon_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub


sub FG_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape,iQuality(2)
	'on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oShape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_orientation")<>"" then oShape.objects("WdgB").rotation=oShape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_orientation")
	set DetBody=oShape.objects("DetBody")
	if typename(oShape.objects("TxtMos"))<>"Nothing" then set DetMOS=oShape.objects("TxtMos")
	iQuality(0)=oSource.quality
	if instr(lcase(oShape.src),"nm_fgs")=0 then
		iQuality(1)=oSource.dataquality("TagnameInh.cp_inh_pv")
		inh_OP=oSource.datavalue("TagnameInh.cp_inh_op")
		inh_PV=oSource.datavalue("TagnameInh.cp_inh_pv")
	else
		iQuality(1)=33216
        inh_OP=3
		inh_PV=3
	end if
	for j=0 to 0
		ipQuality = 4
		ipQuality = iQuality(j)
		if (ipQuality and 8)=8 then
      		sOffScan="Active"
			elseif (ipQuality and 4)=4 then
      		sBad="Bad"
    	end if
	next
	if sOffScan="Active" then
	  	oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
		DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhPV")
		index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
		DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & index
		if inh_OP=1 then
			index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhOP")
			DetMOS.styleclass="TxtMos_" & index
		end if
		if inh_PV=1 then
			index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhPV")
         	DetMOS.styleclass="TxtMos_" & index
        end if
		if inh_PV=0 then
         	DetMOS.styleclass="TxtMos_FG_NotInhibit"
        end if
	elseif sBad="Bad"then
	    oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
  		DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")
	else
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
		DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & index
		if inh_OP=1 then
			index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhOP")
			DetMOS.styleclass="TxtMos_" & index
		end if
		if inh_PV=1 then
			index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhPV")
         	DetMOS.styleclass="TxtMos_" & index
        end if
		if inh_PV=0 then
         	DetMOS.styleclass="TxtMos_FG_NotInhibit"
        end if
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " FG_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub


'*******************************************************************************************
'Channels and RTUs
'*******************************************************************************************
sub Scd_ChnRtu_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
    set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oSOurce.value=0 then
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("OvlStsB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("LinScaleB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkA").value="  Lnk"
	else
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("OvlStsB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("LinScaleB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkA").value="Lnk A"
	end if
    if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_BarFail") then
	    oShape.objects("IndbarA").rangeHi=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_BarFail")
	    oShape.objects("IndbarB").rangeHi=oShape.objects("IndbarA").rangeHi
	    oShape.objects("IndbarA").rangeLo=0
	    oShape.objects("IndbarB").rangeLo=0
	end if
    if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkABarCnt") then
		oShape.objects("IndbarA").value=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_LnkABarCnt")
	end if
	if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkBBarCnt") then
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").value=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_LnkBBarCnt")
	end if
	if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkStsA") then
		oShape.objects("OvlStsA").styleclass="OvlStsA_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsA") )
		oShape.objects("IndbarA").styleclass="IndbarA_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsA") )
	end if
	if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkStsB") then
		oShape.objects("OvlStsB").styleclass="OvlStsB_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsB") )
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").styleclass="IndbarB_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsB") )
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_ChnRtu_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub Scd_MntOvrKey_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next

'*******************************************************************************************
'Stack Damper
'*******************************************************************************************

sub Scd_Damper_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iPV=oSource.datavalue("TagnameVLV.cp_pv")
	iOP=10
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_eop")<>"" then iOP=oSource.datavalue("tagnameVLV.cp_eop")
	iBadCtlFl=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")
	if iBadCtlFl=1 then iPV=3
	if oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_execstate")=1 then
      oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
    else
	  oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
    end if
	if (oSource.dataquality("tagnameVLV.cp_pv") and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
	    oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.dataquality("tagnameVLV.cp_pv") and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		if oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_execstate")=1 then oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
	else
		if oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_execstate")=1 then oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
    if typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" and iOP<>10 then
		oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass="WdgESD_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOP" & iOP)
	elseif typename(oShape.objects("WdgESD"))<>"Nothing" and iOP=10 then
		oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgESD_OnOffValve_OPNA"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index
    if instr(lcase(index),"bad") then oShape.objects("WdgESD").styleclass = "WdgESD_" & index

    rVal=oSource.value
    rval=-round(rval)*0.9
    if rVal<-90 then
      rVal=-90
    elseif rVal>0 then
      rVal=0
    end if
    set oLinDamper=oShape.objects("LinDamper")
    oLinDamper.style.visibility="hidden"
    oLinDamper.rotation=rval
    oLinDamper.style.visibility="inherit"

	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_Damper_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub</SCRIPT>
